THE CITY · Rich and poor in coins: gpt-oss
The same with gpt-oss. Seventeen rich founders starve while being topped up to 1,000 coins a night: eleven farm for employers who take their harvest, and six die at the Market with their food up for sale. 41 of 50 poor founders starve.
100 founders · 400 days · no government · seed 1 · gptoss120
